&+{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{R}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}){\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}.\end{split}

For the first term, we note that it is equal to
\Phi_{5}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}})
with

\begin{split}{\bm{u}}=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{1/2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}},\quad{\bm{B}}=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{-1/2}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{-1/2}.\end{split}

By Theorem
6
, we have:

\begin{split}|\Phi_{5}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}})-\Psi_{3}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}})|\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\tilde{\Psi}_{3}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}}),\end{split}

where

\begin{split}\Psi_{3}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}})=&{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\\
&+\frac{(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1})}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}},\\
\tilde{\Psi}_{3}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}})=&\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{1/2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\|_{2}^{2}\left\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{-1/2}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{-1/2}\right\|_{op}\\
\leq&\|({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\|_{op}\leq\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\|_{op}.\end{split}

For the second term, by Lemma
1
we have

\begin{split}|{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{R}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{R}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,\mathsf{t}}-\|{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,\mathsf{t}}\|_{2}^{2}\Psi_{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{B}},{\bm{I}})|\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,\mathsf{t}}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\\
\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op},\end{split}

where

\begin{split}\Psi_{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{B}},{\bm{I}})=&\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{2}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}=\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}.\end{split}

Note that

\begin{split}(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}=&(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
=&(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\|{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\|_{2}^{2}.\end{split}

Thus, we have

\begin{split}\Psi_{3}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};&{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}})+\Psi_{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{B}},{\bm{I}})\\
=&{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\\
&+\frac{(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.\end{split}

The third term concentrates to zero, and by Lemma
1
, we have

\begin{split}|{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{R}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}){\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}|\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,\mathsf{t}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\\
\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\|_{op}.\end{split}

Next, we do the deterministic equivalent w.r.t.
{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}},
and we have

\begin{split}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}&(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\\
=&{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
=&{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
=&\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}-2(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
&+(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.\end{split}

The second term satisfies

\begin{split}|(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}&({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}|\\
\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\sqrt{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}.\end{split}

The third term can be written as

\begin{split}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}=\Phi_{3}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}},p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}),\end{split}

with
{\bm{A}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2},{\bm{B}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}.

By Theorem
6
, we have

\begin{split}|(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}\Phi_{3}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})-\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\Psi_{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})|\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\widetilde{\Psi}_{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}}),\end{split}

where

\begin{split}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\Psi_{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})=&\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{p_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
=&\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*},\\
\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\widetilde{\Psi}_{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})=&\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}\|_{op}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{p_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
=&\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}\|_{op}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.\end{split}

Combining all terms, we have

\begin{split}&\left|{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\frac{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\right.\\
&\left.-{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right|\\
\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\tilde{T}_{q,1},\end{split}

where

\begin{split}\tilde{T}_{q,1}=&\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}\|_{op}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
&+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\sqrt{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}.\end{split}

For the term
\frac{(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1})}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}},
we first note that from
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof of Theorem B.12)
, we have:

\begin{split}&\left|\frac{(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1})}-\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\right|\\
&\hskip 100.00015pt\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\end{split}

with

\begin{split}\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}=\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\chi(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}).\end{split}

We then do the deterministic equivalent of
{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}).
We have

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})=&{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1})-(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}){\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})\end{split}

For the first term, we decompose

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1})=&{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0})-(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}){\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}),\end{split}

and by Theorem
5
we have

\begin{split}|(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}){\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1})-\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})|\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}).\end{split}

For the second term, from Theorem
5
we have

\begin{split}&\left|(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}){\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})-(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{p_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}\right|\\
&\hskip 100.00015pt\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{p_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}.\end{split}

Using the same arguments in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof of Lemma B.14)
, we have

\begin{split}(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{p_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}\leq\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}))\leq\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}).\end{split}

Thus, combining all terms, we obtain

\begin{split}&\left|{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})-n_{\mathsf{t}}\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})\right|\\
\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}),\end{split}

(21)

which implies that

\begin{split}&\left|\frac{(p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1})}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{\top}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}-\frac{\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})\right|\\
&\hskip 100.00015pt\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}).\end{split}

Cross term.

Let us now consider the following cross term:

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\rangle\\
&+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{R}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\beta}}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}},\end{split}

with
{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}={\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-1}
.

For the first term, we write

\begin{split}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\lambda_{\mathsf{t}})^{-1}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\rangle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\Phi_{2}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}),\end{split}

with

\begin{split}{\bm{u}}=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{1/2}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}},\quad{\bm{v}}=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}^{-1/2}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*},\end{split}

and by Theorem
6
we obtain that

\begin{split}|\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\Phi_{2}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\Psi_{1}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top})|\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}),\end{split}

with

\begin{split}\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\Psi_{1}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top})=&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},1})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\rangle,\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_{\mathsf{t}}}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top})=&\|({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}})\|_{2}\sqrt{\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle}\\
&\vee\sqrt{\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},1})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle}.\end{split}

Then, note that

\begin{split}\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},1})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathsf{t}}{\bm{\beta}}_{{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}\rangle=&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle\\
=&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle.\end{split}

Again by Theorem
5
, we have

\begin{split}|\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}&{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle-\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle|\\
\leq&C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\sqrt{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}.\end{split}

Combining all the terms.

We define

\begin{split}\mathsf{B}_{\mathsf{s},bias}=&\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}+{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
&+\frac{\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\\
&-2{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
&={\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Lambda}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*},\end{split}

with

\begin{split}{\bm{\Lambda}}=&{\bm{I}}-2{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-1}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}+{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}\\
&+\frac{\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}\\
&+\left[\frac{\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}+\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\right]\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2},\end{split}

and the approximation error is given by

\begin{split}\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{\mathsf{s},bias}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}})=&\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\|_{op}\\
&+\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}\\
&+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}\|_{op}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{2}\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}){\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\\
&+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\sqrt{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}\\
&+\frac{\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})\\
&+\|({\bm{I}}-\mathtt{P}_{\perp,{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}})\|_{2}\sqrt{\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle}\\
&\vee\sqrt{\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},1})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},1})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle}\\
&+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\sqrt{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}.\end{split}

Thus, we have

\begin{split}|\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{s},bias}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}})-\overline{\mathsf{B}}_{\mathsf{s},bias}|\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{\mathsf{s},bias}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}).\end{split}

Bounding the approximation error.

It remains to bound
\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{\mathsf{s},bias}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}).
We first note that from the previous computation we have

\begin{split}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}\leq C_{*}\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})},\end{split}

and this is the only term that is not necessarily upper bounded by
C_{*}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}(\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\|_{op}\vee 1)
. Thus, we write

\begin{split}\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{\mathsf{s},bias}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}})\leq\tilde{\mathsf{B}}_{\mathsf{s},bias}:=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|_{2}^{2}(\|{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\|_{op}\vee 1)+\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}.\end{split}

A.2.2
Terms depending on the teacher variance

In this part, we show the deterministic equivalent of
\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{t},var}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}}).

From Theorem
5
, we immediately have

\begin{split}|\mathcal{B}_{\mathsf{t},var}({\bm{Z}}_{\mathsf{t}})-\tau_{\mathsf{t}}^{2}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}|\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}\tau_{\mathsf{t}}^{2}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}.\end{split}

Using (
21
), we obtain that

\begin{split}&\left|\tau_{\mathsf{t}}^{2}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}-{\rm Tr}(({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}})^{2}({\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}^{\top}{\bm{F}}_{\mathsf{t}}+p_{\mathsf{t}}\mu_{\mathsf{t},1})^{-2})}-\tau_{\mathsf{t}}^{2}\frac{\Upsilon({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0};\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})}\right|\\
&\hskip 100.00015pt\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\frac{\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})}{1-\Upsilon(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})},\end{split}

which concludes the proof of Theorem
2
.

Appendix B
Basic deterministic equivalents

We consider a feature vector
{\bm{x}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, with
d\in{\mathbb{N}}\cup\{\infty\}
. Denote by
{\bm{\Sigma}}=\mathbb{E}[{\bm{x}}{\bm{x}}^{\mathsf{T}}]
the covariance of
{\bm{x}}
and by
\xi_{1}^{2}\geq\xi_{2}^{2}\geq\xi_{3}^{2}\geq\cdots
the eigenvalues of
{\bm{\Sigma}}
in non-increasing order. When
d=\infty
, we assume that
{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}})<\infty
.

We assume that the feature
{\bm{x}}
satisfies the following Hanson-Wright-type inequality:

Assumption 3

(Feature concentration)
.

There exist
{\sf c}_{x},{\sf C}_{x}>0
such that for any p.s.d. matrix
{\bm{A}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
with
{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{A}})<\infty
, we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|{\bm{x}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{x}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{A}})\right|\geq t\cdot\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\|_{F}\right)\leq{\sf C}_{x}\exp\left\{-{\sf c}_{x}t\right\}.

(22)

Definition 3

(Intrinsic dimension)
.

For a covariance matrix
{\bm{\Sigma}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
with eigenvalues in nonincreasing order
\xi_{1}^{2}\geq\xi_{2}^{2}\geq\xi_{3}^{2}\geq\cdots
, we define the
intrinsic dimension

r_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}(k)
at level
k\in{\mathbb{N}}
of
{\bm{\Sigma}}
to be the intrinsic dimension of the covariance matrix
{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\geq k}={\rm diag}(\xi_{k}^{2},\xi_{k+1}^{2},\ldots)
, i.e., the covariance matrix projected orthogonally to the top
k-1
eigenspaces, which is given by

r_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}(k):=\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}_{\geq k})}{\|{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\geq k}\|_{\rm op}}=\frac{\sum_{j=k}^{d}\xi^{2}_{j}}{\xi^{2}_{k}}.

Let
\lambda_{*}>0
be the unique positive solution of the following self-consistency equation

n-\frac{\lambda}{\lambda_{*}}={\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}).

We further introduce
\mu_{*}=\frac{\lambda}{\lambda_{*}}
.

The rate of approximation will depend on the following quantity:

\rho_{\lambda}(k)\coloneqq 1+\frac{k\cdot\xi^{2}_{\lfloor\eta_{*}\cdot k\rfloor}}{\lambda}\left\{1+\frac{r_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}(\lfloor\eta_{*}\cdot k\rfloor)\vee k}{k}\log\left(r_{{\bm{\Sigma}}}(\lfloor\eta_{*}\cdot k\rfloor)\vee k\right)\right\},

where
\eta_{*}\in(0,1/2)
is a constant that will only depend on the constants in Assumption
3
.

We will consider the following functionals of the feature matrix:

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda):=

\displaystyle~{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right),

(23)

\displaystyle\Phi_{2}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda):=

\displaystyle~{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right),

(24)

\displaystyle\Phi_{3}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}},\lambda):=

\displaystyle~{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right),

(25)

\displaystyle\Phi_{4}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda):=

\displaystyle~{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}\frac{{\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}}{n}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\right),

(26)

\displaystyle\Phi_{5}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}},\lambda):=

\displaystyle~{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\mathsf{T}}{\bm{X}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\right).

(27)

The deterministic equivalents of these functionals can be written in terms of:

\displaystyle\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}}):=

\displaystyle~{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}\right),

(28)

\displaystyle\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}}):=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{n^{2}}\cdot{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}\right)

(29)

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\cdot\frac{{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}\right){\rm Tr}\left({\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}\right)}{n-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2})},

(30)

\displaystyle\Psi_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}}):=

\displaystyle~{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1})

(31)

\displaystyle~+\frac{\lambda_{*}^{2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}){\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2})}{n-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2})}.

(32)

Note that when
{\bm{B}}={\bm{I}}
, the second functional simplifies to

\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}})=\frac{1}{n}\cdot\frac{{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}\right)}{n-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2})}.

We also define the following approximation functionals:

\begin{split}\widetilde{\Psi}_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}):=&~\sqrt{\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}^{2}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}{\bm{v}}}\vee\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}{\bm{v}}},\\
\widetilde{\Psi}_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}}):=&~\frac{1}{n^{2}}\cdot\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}{\bm{v}}}\|{\bm{B}}^{1/2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-1}{\bm{B}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\\
&~+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\cdot\frac{{\rm Tr}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}\right){\rm Tr}\left({\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2}\right)}{n-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda_{*})^{-2})},\\
\widetilde{\Psi}_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\mathsf{T}},{\bm{B}}):=&~\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}.\end{split}

Theorem 5
.

(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Theorem 6,8,9)

Assume the features
({\bm{x}}_{i})_{i\in[n]}
satisfy Assumption
3
with some constants
{\sf c}_{x},{\sf C}_{x}>0
. For any
D,K>0
, there exist constants
\eta:=\eta_{x}\in(0,1/2)
(only depending on
{\sf c}_{x},{\sf C}_{x}
),
C_{D,K}>0
(only depending on
D,K
), and
C_{x,K,D}>0
(only depending on
{\sf c}_{x},{\sf C}_{x},D,K
) such that the following holds. For all
n\geq C_{D,K}
and
\lambda>0
, if it holds that

\lambda\cdot\rho_{\lambda}(n)\geq\|{\bm{\Sigma}}\|_{\rm op}\cdot n^{-K},\qquad\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{3/2}(n)\leq K\sqrt{n},

(33)

then for any p.s.d. matrices
{\bm{A}}
, we have with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
that

\displaystyle\big|\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda)-\frac{\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}})\big|\leq

\displaystyle~C_{x,D,K}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{3/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\frac{\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}}),

(34)

\displaystyle\big|\Phi_{3}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}},\lambda)-\left(\frac{n\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\right)^{2}\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}})\big|\leq

\displaystyle~C_{x,D,K}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{6}\log^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\frac{n\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\right)^{2}\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}}),

(35)

\displaystyle\big|\Phi_{4}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda)-\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}})\big|\leq

\displaystyle~C_{x,D,K}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{6}\log^{3/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}}).

(36)

The deterministic equivalents above assume that
{\bm{A}}
is a p.s.d. matrix. In next theorem, instead we take
{\bm{A}}={\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}
, which is a non-p.s.d. rank-
1
matrix.

Theorem 6
.

Under the same assumptions of Theorem
5
, let
{\bm{A}}={\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}
and
{\bm{B}}
be a p.s.d. matrix. Then, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda)-\frac{\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}})\right|\leq

\displaystyle~C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{3/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\sqrt{\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top})\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top})},

(37)

\displaystyle\big|\Phi_{2}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda)-\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}})\big|\leq

\displaystyle~C_{x,D,K}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{3/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}),

(38)

\displaystyle\big|\Phi_{3}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}},\lambda)-\left(\frac{n\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\right)^{2}\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})\big|\leq

\displaystyle~C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)\log^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\left(\frac{n\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\right)^{2}\widetilde{\Psi}_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}}),

(39)

\displaystyle\big|\Phi_{5}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}},\lambda)-\Psi_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})\big|\leq

\displaystyle~C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)\log^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\Psi}_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}}).

(40)

Lemma 1
.

Under the same assumptions of Theorem
6
, consider a deterministic vector
{\bm{v}}
and a random vector
{\bm{u}}
with i.i.d. entries
u_{i}
such that
\mathbb{E}[u_{i}]=0,\mathbb{E}[u_{i}^{2}]=1,
and
\mathbb{E}[{\bm{x}}_{i}u_{i}]=0.
Then, with probability at least
1-n^{-D},
we have

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle|\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{v}}\|_{2}

(41)

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{v}},{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{B}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle|\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{op}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{v}}\|_{2}

(42)

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{B}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle-n\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2})|\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho^{6}_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}\|_{op}.

(43)

The proof of Lemma
1
is deferred to Appendix
D
.

Appendix C
Proof of Theorem
6
: Proof of new deterministic equivalent

We introduce the same notations as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Appendix A)
. We first define

\begin{split}&{\bm{M}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2},\quad\overline{{\bm{M}}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}(\mu_{*}{\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}.\end{split}

Then, we define some notation for the leave-one-out arguments. Recalling
{\bm{X}}=[{\bm{x}}_{1},\dots,{\bm{x}}_{n}]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}
, we define
{\bm{X}}_{i}=[{\bm{x}}_{1},\dots,{\bm{x}}_{i-1},{\bm{x}}_{i+1},\dots,{\bm{x}}_{n}]^{\top}
and

\begin{split}{\bm{M}}_{-i}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{i}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2},\quad\overline{{\bm{M}}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\left(\frac{n}{1+\kappa}{\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2},\quad\kappa=\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i})].\end{split}

When the subscript
i
is not important, we write
{\bm{M}}_{-i}
as
{\bm{M}}_{-}
for short.

C.1
General procedure of proving deterministic equivalent

We follow the same approach as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Theorem 6)
. We take the proof of
\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)
as an illustrative example for the proof techniques, and the argument for the other functionals is analogous.

We first decompose the difference between the functional and its deterministic equivalent into two parts:

\begin{split}|\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)&-\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top})|\\
\leq&\underbrace{|\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)]|}_{\text{Martingale part}}+\underbrace{|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)]-\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top})|}_{\text{Deterministic part}}.\end{split}

(44)

Then, we bound the two parts separately. For the deterministic part, we directly prove

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)]-\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top})|\leq C_{*}{\mathcal{E}}_{n}\tilde{\Psi}_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}),\end{split}

with
{\mathcal{E}}_{n}=\tilde{\mathcal{O}}_{n}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

For the martingale part, we decompose the difference into a martingale difference sequence, and show high probability bounds for the martingale difference term, as described in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 3)
. We briefly summarize the steps for completeness. In particular, we define

\begin{split}S_{n}:=\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)]=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1})\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top},\lambda)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i},\end{split}

where
\mathbb{E}_{i}
denote the partial expectation w.r.t.
\{{\bm{x}}_{i+1},\dots{\bm{x}}_{n}\}.
For any constant
R,
we define the truncated martingale sequence and the remainder as

\begin{split}&\tilde{S}_{n}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\bm{1}_{\Delta_{i}\in[-R,R]}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\Delta_{i}\bm{1}_{\Delta_{i}\in[-R,R]}],\\
&R_{n}:=S_{n}-\tilde{S}_{n}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\bm{1}_{\Delta_{i}\notin[-R,R]}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\Delta_{i}\bm{1}_{\Delta_{i}\notin[-R,R]}].\end{split}

By Azuma-Hoeffding inequality, we have

\begin{split}\Pr[|\tilde{S}_{n}|\geq t]\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2nR^{2}}\right).\end{split}

Thus, there exists a constant
C_{*}
such that
|\tilde{S}_{n}|\leq C_{*}\sqrt{n\log(n)}R
with probability at least
1-n^{-D}.
Next, we show that, with probability at least
1-n^{D}
,

\begin{split}|\Delta_{i}|=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),\quad\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\Delta_{i}^{2}]^{1/2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),\end{split}

which implies
R_{n}=0
with probability at least
1-n^{-D},
and finishes the proof.

C.2
Deterministic equivalent of
{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}})

C.2.1
Bound the Deterministic part

Consider
{\bm{A}}={\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\mathsf{T}}
.
By following the same steps as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 2)
, we obtain that

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}})]-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})|&=|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}(\mathbb{E}[{\bm{M}}]-\overline{{\bm{M}}}))|\\
&\leq|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}(\mathbb{E}[{\bm{M}}]-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}))|+|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}))|.\end{split}

(45)

Bounding the term
|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}(\mathbb{E}[{\bm{M}}]-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}))|
.
We decompose the term as

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}))]|\leq n|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\Delta_{2})]|+n|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\Delta_{3})]|,\end{split}

where

\begin{split}\Delta_{2}&:=\frac{{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}}{(1+\kappa)(1+{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}})}({\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}-\kappa),\\
\Delta_{3}&:=-\frac{{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}}{1+{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}}({\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}-\kappa).\end{split}

To bound
n|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\Delta_{2})]|,
we bound the numerator as

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}({\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}-\kappa)|]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{z}})^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{M}}_{-}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}}\left[({\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}})^{3}\right]^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{M}}_{-}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}}\left[({\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}-\kappa)^{3}\right]^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

We can bound each term using
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 2, Lemma 4)
as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 2)
:

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{z}})^{3}]^{1/3}\leq C_{*}\sqrt{\frac{(1+\kappa)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n}{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})},\\
&\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{M}}_{-}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}}\left[({\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}})^{3}\right]^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})},\\
&\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{M}}_{-}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\bm{z}}\left[({\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}-\kappa)^{3}\right]^{\frac{2}{3}}\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}.\end{split}

Combining all the terms, we have that

\begin{split}n|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\Delta_{2})]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.\end{split}

To bound
n|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\Delta_{3})]|,
we follow the procedure in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, eq (79))
:

\begin{split}n|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\Delta_{3})]|\leq&n\mathbb{E}[({\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}})^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}})^{2}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{3}}{n}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}\end{split}.

Combining the two above display, we have

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}))]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.\end{split}

(46)

Bounding the term
|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}))|
.
We note that

\begin{split}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}))|=&n|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})|\cdot\frac{|\kappa-{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}})|}{(1+\kappa)(1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}))}\\
\leq&n\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}\cdot\frac{|\kappa-{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}})|}{(1+\kappa)(1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}))}\\
\leq&\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}\cdot\frac{|\kappa-{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}})|}{1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}})},\end{split}

where in the first inequality we use Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and that
\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-},{\bm{\Sigma}},\overline{{\bm{M}}}
commute, and in the second inequality we use that
\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}\|_{op}\leq\frac{1+\kappa}{n}.

Then we follow the same steps as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 2)
, and obtain that

\begin{split}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}))|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.\end{split}

(47)

Combining (
45
), (
46
), (
47
) we have

|\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}})]-{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.

(48)

C.2.2
Bounding the martingale part

Following the proof in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 3)
, we decompose the martingale parts as follows:

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}})-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}})]=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i},\end{split}

where

\Delta_{i}=(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1}){\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}({\bm{M}}-{\bm{M}}_{-i})).

Bounding
|\Delta_{i}|
with high probability.
We decompose
{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}({\bm{M}}-{\bm{M}}_{-i}))
in the same way as
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, eq (86))
, and we get

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}({\bm{M}}-{\bm{M}}_{-i}))=\frac{{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}{1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i})}-\frac{{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}({\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i}))}{(1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i}))(1+{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i})}.\end{split}

For the first term, we have

\begin{split}(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1}){\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}&=\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{z}}_{i}}[{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}]]\\
&=\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i})].\end{split}

By Lemma
2
, we have that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i})|]\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\varphi_{1}(p)\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-i}\left(\frac{{\bm{A}}+{\bm{A}}^{\top}}{2}\right){\bm{M}}_{-i})\|_{F}]\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\varphi_{1}(d)\mathbb{E}_{i}[(\|{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}\|_{F}])].\end{split}

Then, we write

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[(\|{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i})\|_{F}]\leq&\mathbb{E}_{i}[(\|{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i})\|_{F}^{2}]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}^{2}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}^{2})]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}^{2})^{2}]^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}^{2})^{2}]^{\frac{1}{4}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}}{n}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})},\end{split}

where in the last step we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4.(b))
, and the same bound holds for
\|{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}\|_{F}.
Thus we obtain for the first term that

\begin{split}(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1}){\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}}{n}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.\end{split}

For the second term, for
j\in\{i,i-1\}
we similarly have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{j}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}&{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}({\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i}))|]\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}_{j}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i})|^{2}]^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

Again by Lemma
2
, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{\frac{1}{2}}&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}^{2}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}^{2})]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}}{n}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})},\end{split}

where in the first inequality we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4.(b))
.

Following the same steps as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 3)
, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-i})|^{2}]^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)\log(n)}{n},\end{split}

and the same bounds hold for
\mathbb{E}_{i-1}
without the factor
\log(n).
By combining the above bounds and apply a union bound to
\Delta_{i},i\in[n],
we have that

|\Delta_{i}|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)\log(n)}{n}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.

The rest of the proof is exactly the same as Steps 3, 4 in the Proof of Proposition 3 of
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
)
, and the only difference is the following bound:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\Delta_{i}^{2}]^{1/2}&\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}})^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i})\right]^{1/2}\\
&\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i})^{2}\right]^{\frac{1}{4}}\\
&\leq C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}(n)\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.\end{split}

Combining all the bounds and following the same steps as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Steps 3, 4 in the Proof of Proposition 3)
, we have

\begin{split}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}})-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}})]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}\log^{\frac{3}{2}}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}){\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}})}.\end{split}

(49)

Combining all the bounds.
Combining (
44
), (
48
), (
49
), we obtain

\left|\Phi_{1}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},\lambda)-\frac{\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}})\right|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{\frac{3}{2}}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\lambda_{*}}{\lambda}\sqrt{\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top})\Psi_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top})}.

(50)

C.3
Deterministic equivalent of
{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})

C.3.1
Bounding the deterministic part

By using exchangeability and Sherman-Morrison formula, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{v}}]&=n\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}]\\
&=n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}{1+\kappa}\right]+n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1}){\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}{(1+\kappa)(1+S_{1})}\right]\\
&=n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}{1+\kappa}\right]+n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1}){\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}{(1+\kappa)(1+S_{1})}\right].\end{split}

For the first term, we note that (denoting
\tilde{\mu}_{-}=n/(1+\kappa)
)

\begin{split}n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}{1+\kappa}\right]=\tilde{\mu}_{-}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}],\end{split}

and we have

\begin{split}&|\tilde{\mu}_{-}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}]-\mu_{*}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}]|\\
&\leq\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{-}}{\mu_{*}}\mu_{*}|\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}]-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}]|+\frac{|\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*}|}{\mu_{*}}\mu_{*}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}]\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{3/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mu_{*}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}+\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\mu_{*}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}],\end{split}

(51)

where we use the fact that

\begin{split}\frac{|\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*}|}{\mu_{*}}\leq\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\end{split}

proved in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 6)
.

For the second term, we have

\begin{split}n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1}){\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}{(1+\kappa)(1+S_{1})}\right]&\leq\tilde{\mu}_{-}\mathbb{E}[(\kappa-S_{1})^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq\mu_{*}\mathbb{E}[(\kappa-S_{1})^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&\qquad+\frac{|\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*}|}{\mu_{*}}\tilde{\mu}_{-}\mathbb{E}[(\kappa-S_{1})^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mu_{*}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\|_{F}]\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mu_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\mathbb{E}[\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}}]\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mu_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\mu_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

(52)

Combining (
51
), (
52
), we have

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}]-\mu_{*}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}|C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{3/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}}).\end{split}

(53)

C.3.2
Bounding the martingale part

Following the same methods, we have

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})]=\Sigma_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i},\end{split}

with
\Delta_{i}=(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1}){\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}).

We first have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\Delta_{i}^{2}]^{1/2}\leq&2n\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[\frac{({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{2}}{(1+S_{1})^{2}}\right]^{1/2}\\
&\leq 4\frac{n}{(1+\kappa)}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{2}\right]^{1/2}+\mathbb{E}_{i-1}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1})(2+\kappa+S_{1})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{2}}{(1+S_{1})^{2}}\right]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\tilde{\mu}_{-}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}]^{1/2}\quad\text{ (note that the second term has lower order due to the extra $\kappa-S_{1}$)}\\
&\leq C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}^{3/2}(n)\mu_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

Next, we have

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}{\bm{M}}_{-i})=&\frac{1}{(1+S_{i})}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{I}}-{\bm{Z}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}{\bm{M}}_{-i}){\bm{z}}_{i}\\
=&\lambda\frac{1}{(1+S_{i})}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i},\end{split}

which implies that

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})&-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}{\bm{M}}_{-i})]\\
=&\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{i}\left[\lambda\frac{n}{(1+S_{i})}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}\right]\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{1}{n}\left(\lambda\frac{n}{1+\kappa}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}]\right)\quad\text{(we ignore the term with the extra $\kappa-S_{1}$ factor as it is of lower order)}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{1}{n}\left(\lambda\tilde{\mu}_{-}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i})]^{1/2}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-2}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i}]^{1/2}\right)\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n}\mu_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}},\end{split}

where we use
{\bm{M}}_{i}\preceq\lambda^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}.

Combining all the bounds and following the same steps described in Appendix
C.1
, we have

\begin{split}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})-\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mu_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

(54)

Combining (
53
), (
54
), we have that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}})-\mu_{*}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}|\leq\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\log^{3/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{I}}).\end{split}

C.4
Deterministic equivalent of
{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})

C.4.1
Bounding the deterministic part

We write
\Phi_{3}({\bm{X}};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}},\lambda)={\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})
, and recall that
{\bm{A}}={\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}.

We first decompose
\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})-{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})]
as

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}&{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})-{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})]\\
=&\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}){\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}]+\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{B}}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})]}_{T_{1}({\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}}[{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}){\bm{B}}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})]}_{T_{2}({\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})}.\end{split}

(55)

We have the following claims, which we will prove later.

Claim 1
.

|T_{1}({\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{7/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}

Claim 2
.

|T_{2}({\bm{u}},{\bm{v}})-\frac{n\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})]}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-})|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{5}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}{\bm{v}}}}

We first prove the main deterministic bounds using the above claims. Combining Claim
1
, Claim
2
and (
55
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})]-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})-\frac{n}{(1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}))^{2}}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}){\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{M}^{2})}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left|\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})]-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})-\frac{n}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})]{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2})\right|

(56)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})|

(57)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left|\frac{n{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2})}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}-\frac{n{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{M}^{2})}{(1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}))^{2}}\right|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})]

(58)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left|\frac{n{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{M}^{2})}{(1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}))^{2}}\right|\left|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})]-\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{M}^{2})}\right|.

(59)

For (
56
), we use Claim
1
and Claim
2
, to obtain that

\begin{split}\eqref{eq:deteq-phi3-det-decomp-1}\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{5}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

For (
57
), we first note that

\begin{split}\overline{{\bm{M}}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}&=\overline{{\bm{M}}}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda)^{-1}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*}){\bm{\Sigma}}\\
&=\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda)^{-1}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*}){\bm{\Sigma}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}.\end{split}

Thus, we have

\begin{split}\eqref{eq:deteq-phi3-det-decomp-2}=&|2{\bm{u}}^{\top}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}){\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}+{\bm{u}}^{\top}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}){\bm{B}}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}){\bm{v}}|\\
\leq&2\|(\tilde{\mu}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda)^{-1}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*})\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}\\
&+\|(\tilde{\mu}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda)^{-1}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*})\|_{op}^{2}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{bu^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

For (
58
), we first note that

\begin{split}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}=\overline{{\bm{M}}}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}{\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda)^{-2}(\tilde{\mu}_{-}-\mu_{*}){\bm{\Sigma}}((\tilde{\mu}_{-}+\mu_{*}){\bm{\Sigma}}+\lambda)\overline{{\bm{M}}},\end{split}

which implies that

\begin{split}\left|\frac{n{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2})}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}-\frac{n{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{M}^{2})}{(1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}))^{2}}\right|=&\left|\frac{n{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}-\frac{n{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}{(1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}))^{2}}\right|\\
\leq&\left|\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{-}^{2}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}}{n}-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}{n}\right|,\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}n\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{7/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

Thus, we have

\begin{split}\eqref{eq:deteq-phi3-det-decomp-3}\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{9/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}},\end{split}

by using that
\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})]\leq\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})]\leq\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})^{2}]^{1/2},
and then using Lemma
4
and
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4.(b))
.

For (
59
), we simply use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proposition 4)
, and we have

\begin{split}\eqref{eq:deteq-phi3-det-decomp-4}\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)\log^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}){\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}.\end{split}

By combining the upper bounds for (
56
)-(
59
), we have

\begin{split}&\left|\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})]-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}){\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{M}^{2})}\right|\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)\log^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\tilde{\Psi}_{2}(\lambda_{*},{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}}).\end{split}

(60)

It remains to prove Claim
1
and Claim
2
.

Proof of Claim
1
.

It is sufficient to bound
\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{X}}}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}){\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})],
and the other one holds by symmetry. We note that

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}){\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})={\rm Tr}({\bm{w}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}({\bm{M}}-\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-})),\end{split}

with
{\bm{w}}={\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{v}}.
Following the same steps as in
C.2.1
, we have

\begin{split}|T_{1}({\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})|\leq&\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}\\
\leq&\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{v}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{1/2}\|_{op}}\\
\leq&\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{3}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}\|{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}\|_{op}\|{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}\|_{op}}\\
\leq&\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{9/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op},\end{split}

where in the last step we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4.(b))
.
∎

Proof of Claim
2
.

For the second term, we use the same decomposition as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
. In particular, we have:

\begin{split}T_{2}({\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})&=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}}\left[{\rm Tr}\left(\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}\left(\frac{n{\bm{I}}}{1+\kappa}-{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}\right){\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}\left(\frac{n{\bm{I}}}{1+\kappa}-{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}\right)\right)\right]\\
&=(I)+(II),\end{split}

where

\begin{split}&(I)=n\mathbb{E}\sum_{i,j\in[3]}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{1i}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{1j}),\\
&(II)=n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\sum_{i,j\in[3]}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{1i}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-2}\Delta_{2j}),\end{split}

and using the same notation as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
we define

\begin{split}&\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}=\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-},\qquad{\bm{Q}}=\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}},\\
&\Delta_{i1}=\frac{{\bm{I}}-{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}}{1+\kappa},\quad\Delta_{i2}\frac{{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}(S_{i}-\kappa)}{(1+S_{i})(1+\kappa)},\quad\Delta_{i3}=-\frac{-{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}}{1+S_{i}}.\end{split}

Bounding
\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{11})]

We expand the term in the same way as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
and we have

\begin{split}(1+\kappa)^{2}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{11})]=-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1})]+\mathbb{E}[{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1}].\end{split}

Furthermore, we have

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1})]|\leq&\mathbb{E}[\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{v}}}]\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}\|_{op}]\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{4}(n)}{n^{2}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}},\end{split}

where in the last step we use Lemma
4
and
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4.(a))
.

For the second term, we use

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}&{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1}]-{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-})\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}^{2})]|\\
=&\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}))({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1}-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}^{2})])]\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}))^{2}]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1}-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}^{2})])^{2}]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\|\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\|_{F}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1})\|_{F}]\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\|\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\|_{F}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-1}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-1}\|_{F}^{2}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho^{2}_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}\\
\leq&\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho^{5/2}_{\lambda}(n)}{n^{3/2}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

Furthermore, we have

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-})|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})]-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})]|\leq&C_{*}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}}|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}({\bm{M}}^{2}-{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})]|\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}}{n}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}({\bm{M}}^{2}-{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})]|.\end{split}

We bound
|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}({\bm{M}}^{2}-{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})]|
by first noting that

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}({\bm{M}}^{2}-{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2}))]=&\mathbb{E}[{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{z}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}-2{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{z}}]\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{z}})^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}})^{2}]^{1/2}+2\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{z}}|]\\
\leq&C_{*}(\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})]\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-})]+\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})])\\
\leq&\frac{C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}^{4}(n)}{n}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op},\end{split}

where we use

{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-})\leq\|{\bm{M}}_{-}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{1/2}\|_{op}\|{\bm{M}}_{-}\|_{F},\qquad{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})\leq\|{\bm{M}}_{-}\|_{op}\|{\bm{M}}_{-}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{1/2}\|_{op}\|{\bm{M}}_{-}\|_{F},

and then apply Lemma
4
and
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4)
.

Thus, combining the two parts, we have:

\begin{split}&|n\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{11})]-\frac{n}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}{\rm Tr}(\overline{Q}_{-}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})])|\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho^{5/2}_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

Bounding
\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{12})]

We expand the term as follows:
