\begin{split}(1+\kappa)^{2}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{11})]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top})(S_{1}-\kappa)}{(1+S_{1})}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top})(S_{1}-\kappa)}{(1+S_{1})}\right].\end{split}

For the first term, we have

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top})(S_{1}-\kappa)}{(1+S_{1})}\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}[(S_{1}-\kappa)^{3}]^{\frac{1}{3}}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{v}})^{3}]^{\frac{1}{3}}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{u}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{3}]^{1/3}\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}[\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}({\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-})^{2}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}{\bm{u}}}]\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}\|_{op}]\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{u}}}\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{4}}{n^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}}.\end{split}

For the second term, we have

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top})(S_{1}-\kappa)}{(1+S_{1})}\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}[(S_{1}-\kappa)^{3}]^{\frac{1}{3}}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{3}]^{\frac{1}{3}}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{3}]^{\frac{1}{3}}\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-}^{2})]\|\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\|_{F}\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{4}}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}}.\end{split}

Thus, by combining all the terms, we have

\begin{split}|n\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{12})]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{4}}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{u}}}.\end{split}

Bounding
\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{12}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{13})]

We have

\begin{split}|(1+\kappa)^{2}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{12}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{13})]|\leq\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{1})(S_{1}-\kappa)],\end{split}

which is of lower order compared to
\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\Delta_{12})]
, due to the extra
{\bm{M}}_{1}
term.

Bounding
\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-2}\Delta_{21})]

Following the same steps as in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-2}\Delta_{21})]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta_{11}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}\Delta_{12})}{(1+\tilde{S}_{1})(1+\tilde{S}_{2})}\right],\end{split}

and

\begin{split}(1+\kappa)^{2}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}&\Delta^{\top}_{11}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-2}\Delta_{21})\\
=&{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{2}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}))\\
&+{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}-{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}.\end{split}

For the first term, we have

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}&{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{2}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}))]|\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{3}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{2}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-})^{3}]^{1/3}.\end{split}

We first have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{3}]^{1/3}\leq&C_{*}(\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{3}]^{1/3}+\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{3/2}]^{1/3})\\
\leq&C_{*}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}(\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{3}]^{1/3}+\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{{\rm op}}^{3/2}\|{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{3/2}]^{1/3}).\end{split}

The rest of the proof follows the same steps in the corresponding part in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
, and we just note that

\begin{split}\|\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\|_{F}=\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}},\end{split}

which implies that

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}&{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{2}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}))]|\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)}{n^{3/2}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{8}(n)}{n^{5/2}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

The second and the third terms are of lower order compared to the first, term following the same steps as in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
.

Bounding
\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}\Delta^{\top}_{12}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-2}\Delta_{22})]

We follow the same steps as in the corresponding parts in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
, where we define

{\bm{D}}_{i}=\frac{S_{i}-\kappa}{1+S_{i}}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top},\quad\tilde{\bm{D}}_{i}=\frac{\tilde{S}_{i}-\kappa}{1+\tilde{S}_{i}}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}.

Then, the term can be expanded as

\begin{split}(1+\kappa)^{2}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}&\Delta^{\top}_{12}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\Delta_{22})]\\
\leq&{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{\bm{D}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\tilde{\bm{D}}_{2})-2\mathbb{E}[\delta_{1}({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{2}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\tilde{\bm{D}}_{22}\tilde{\bm{Q}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}]\\
&+\mathbb{E}[\delta_{1}\delta_{2}({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{4}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{Q}}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}].\end{split}

Following the same steps as in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof of Claim 2)
, we have

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{Q}}}\tilde{\bm{D}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\tilde{\bm{D}}_{2})]|\leq&\|\overline{{\bm{Q}}}\|_{F}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{op}^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{\bm{D}}\|_{op}^{4}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{4}]^{1/4}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{op}^{4}]^{1/4}\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{\bm{D}}\|_{op}^{4}]^{1/2}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}_{-}^{2}{\bm{v}}}\\
\leq&\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{op}^{4}]^{1/4}\mathbb{E}[\|\tilde{\bm{D}}\|_{op}^{4}]^{1/2}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}},\end{split}

and the remaining steps are the same.

For the rest of the term, we just show that
\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{q}]^{1/q}
can be upper bound similarly to
\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{q}]^{1/q}
with an extra factor of
\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}.

To see this, we have:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{q}]^{1/q}&\leq\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{q}]^{1/q}+\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{q/2}]^{1/q}\\
&\leq\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{3q}]^{1/3q}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{3q}]^{1/3q}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}^{1/2}\|_{F}^{3q}]^{1/3q}\\
&=\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{op}^{3q/2}]^{1/3q}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{3q}]^{1/3q}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{12})^{3q/2}]^{1/3q}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op},\end{split}

where we use Lemma
4
and
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4.(b))
.

Combining all terms.

The rest of the terms, as discussed in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 4)
, are of lower order compared to the terms upper bounded in the previous section. Combining the upper bounds finishes the proof of Claim
2
.

C.4.2
Bounding the martingale part

Define

\begin{split}\Delta_{i}=(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1})({\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})).\end{split}

First, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|\Delta_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq&\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{v}})]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

Next, we show a high probability bound on
|\Delta_{i}|.
We note that

\begin{split}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{v}}-{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}=&-\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}}{1+S_{i}}-\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}}{1+S_{i}}\\
&+\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}}{(1+S_{i})^{2}}.\end{split}

We have that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}\left|\mathbb{E}_{i}\left[\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}}{1+S_{i}}\right]\right|\leq&\mathbb{E}_{i}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i})^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[({\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}})^{2}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{u}}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}_{i}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{2}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{v}}]}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}},\end{split}

where we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 1)
, that
\|{\bm{M}}_{i}\|_{op}\leq C_{*}\ \frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n}
,
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4)
, and Lemma
4
. The same bounds hold for
\mathbb{E}_{i}\left[\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}}{1+S_{i}}\right]
by symmetry of
{\bm{u}},{\bm{v}}.

Next, we have

\begin{split}\left|\mathbb{E}_{i}\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}}{(1+S_{i})^{2}}\right|\leq&\mathbb{E}_{i}[({\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i})^{3/2}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}_{i}[({\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i})^{3/2}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}_{i}[({\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i})^{3}]^{1/3}\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n )\mathbb{E}_{i}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{u}}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{v}}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})]\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n )\rho_{\lambda}^{4}(n)}{n}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}{\bm{v}}}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n )\rho_{\lambda}^{5}(n)}{n}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}{\bm{v}}}},\end{split}

where in the last step we use
{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}})\leq C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}(n).

Thus, we have that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D},

\begin{split}\Delta_{i}\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n )\rho_{\lambda}^{5}(n)}{n}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}{\bm{v}}}}.\end{split}

Then, by following the same steps as in Appendix
C.1
, we obtain that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})]\leq&\frac{\log(n )\rho_{\lambda}^{5}(n)}{n}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}.\end{split}

(61)

Finally, combining (
61
) and (
60
), we obtain that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}})-\Psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}})|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)\log^{5/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\Psi}_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top},{\bm{B}}).\end{split}

∎

C.5
Deterministic equivalent of
{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}){\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}))

For simplicity, we define
{\bm{M}}_{12}
to be
{\bm{M}}
removing
{\bm{x}}_{1},{\bm{x}}_{2}
. We also define

\begin{split}&\kappa=\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-})],\\
&S_{i}={\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{-i}{\bm{z}}_{i},\qquad\tilde{S}_{i}={\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{i},\qquad i\in\{1,2\}.\end{split}

C.5.1
Bounding the deterministic part

By exchangeability, we decompose the term as follows:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}){\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}))]&=n\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}]\\
&\quad+n(n-1)\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}})].\end{split}

Deterministic equivalent of
n\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}]
.

By using Sherman-Morrison formula, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}]=&\frac{\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})]}{(1+S_{1})^{2}}\\
=&\frac{\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})]}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}\\
&+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1})(2+S_{1}+\kappa)}{(1+\kappa)^{2}(1+S_{1})^{2}}({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})\right].\end{split}

For the first term, we have

\begin{split}n&\left|\frac{\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})]}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}-\frac{{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1})]}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}\right|\\
&=n\left|\frac{\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}))]}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}\right|\\
&\leq\frac{1}{n}\tilde{\mu}_{-}^{2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}))^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{-}^{2}}{n}\|{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\|_{F}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}\|_{F}]\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mu_{*}^{2}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}^{1/2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}.\end{split}

Also, from
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proposition 6)
, we obtain that

\begin{split}\left|\frac{n{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1})]}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\frac{{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}\right|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{6}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\frac{{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}.\end{split}

Thus, we have

\begin{split}\left|\frac{n\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})]}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\frac{{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}\right|\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{6}}{\sqrt{n}}\biggl(\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\\
&+\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\frac{{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}\biggr).\end{split}

For the second term, we have

\begin{split}&\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1})(2+S_{1}+\kappa)}{(1+\kappa)^{2}(1+S_{1})^{2}}({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})\right]\right|\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}[(\kappa-S_{1})^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{z}}_{1})^{3}]^{1/3}\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-1}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{-1})]\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op},\end{split}

where in the last step we use Lemma
3
.

Deterministic equivalent of
n(n-1)\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top})]
.

We decompose the term as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top})]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})}\right]

(62)

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\tilde{S}_{1})}\right]

(63)

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\tilde{S}_{2})}\right]

(64)

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\tilde{S}_{1})(1+\tilde{S}_{2})}\right].

(65)

For (
62
), we have

\begin{split}\eqref{eq:Phi5-det-part2-1}&=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})}\right]\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1})(1+\kappa)+(\kappa-S_{2})(1+S_{1})}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\kappa)^{2}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}\right]\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12})}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1})(1+\kappa)+(\kappa-S_{2})(1+S_{1})}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\kappa)^{2}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}\right].\end{split}

For the first term, we have

\begin{split}&\left|n(n-1)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12})}{(1+\kappa)^{2}}\right]-\mu_{*}^{2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}})-\frac{\overline{\mu}_{*}^{4}}{n}\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\overline{\mu}_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}\right|\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{6}}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\mu_{*}^{2}\sqrt{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{v}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}}\frac{\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}}{n}+\frac{\mu_{*}^{4}}{n}\frac{|{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}^{2}{\bm{v}}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}\right).\end{split}

For the second term, we have

\begin{split}n(n-1)&\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1})(1+\kappa)+(\kappa-S_{2})(1+S_{1})}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\kappa)^{2}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}\right]\right|\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}n^{2}\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{1}|^{2}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}n^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}n^{2}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{v}}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{2}]^{1/4}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{2})^{2}]^{1/4}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}n^{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{u}}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}n^{2}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2}\|_{op}^{2}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op},\end{split}

where in the last step we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4.(b))
.

For (
63
) and (
64
), note that
\eqref{eq:Phi5-det-part2-2}=\eqref{eq:Phi5-det-part2-3}
by exchangability of
{\bm{z}}_{1},{\bm{z}}_{2},
and we have

\begin{split}\eqref{eq:Phi5-det-part2-2}=&\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}}{(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\tilde{S}_{1})}\right]\\
=&\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}}{(1+\kappa)^{3}}\right]\\
&+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{(\kappa-S_{1})(1+\kappa)^{2}+(\kappa-S_{2})(1+S_{1})(1+\kappa)+(\kappa-\tilde{S}_{1})(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})}{(1+\kappa)^{3}(1+S_{1})(1+S_{2})(1+\tilde{S}_{1})}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}\right].\end{split}

For the first term, we have

\begin{split}&\left|n^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{2}{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}}{(1+\kappa)^{3}}\right]-\frac{n^{2}}{(1+\kappa)^{3}}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12})]\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2})]\right|\\
&\qquad=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{-}^{3}}{n}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}\right]-\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{-}^{3}}{n}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12})]\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2})]\right|\\
&\qquad\leq n^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}))^{2}\right]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}\left[({\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2})^{2}\right]^{1/2}\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}n^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}\|_{F}^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\mu_{\lambda}^{3}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}.\end{split}

Next, we derive

\begin{split}&\left|\frac{\tilde{\mu}_{-}^{3}}{n}\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12})]\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2})]-\frac{\mu_{*}^{3}}{n}\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}\right|\\
&\qquad\leq n^{2}\left|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12})-{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}]\right|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2})]+n^{2}|\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{v}}]|\left|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{12}^{2})]-\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}\right|\\
&\qquad\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|B\|_{op},\end{split}

where we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proposition 4)
.

For (
65
), we can easily see that it has lower order compared to (
65
), due to an extra
{\bm{z}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{12}{\bm{z}}_{1}
factor.

Finally, by combining the upper bounds for (
62
)–(
65
) and noting that

\begin{split}\Psi_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})=\mu_{*}^{2}{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}\overline{)}+\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}\frac{{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}-2\frac{\mu_{*}^{3}}{n}\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{v}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\mu_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}+\frac{\overline{\mu}_{*}^{4}}{n}\frac{{\bm{u}}^{\top}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2}{\bm{v}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})}{1-\frac{\overline{\mu}_{*}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{2})},\end{split}

we conclude that

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}})]-\Psi_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}})|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\tilde{\Psi}_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}}).\end{split}

C.5.2
Bounding the martingale part

For the martingale part, we first define

\begin{split}\Delta_{i}=(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1}){\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}){\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}){\bm{v}}.\end{split}

We recall that
{\bm{M}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}=({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}+\lambda{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1})^{-1}
and thus
\|{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\|_{op}\leq 1.
It is then easy to see that

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\Delta_{i}^{2}]^{1/2}\leq C_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}.\end{split}

It remains to obtain a high probability bounds on
\Delta_{i}.

Using Sherman-Morrison formula and the fact that
{\bm{I}}-{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}=\lambda{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{M}},
we first compute

\begin{split}{\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}})&{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}({\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}){\bm{v}}-{\bm{u}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}){\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}({\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}){\bm{v}}\\
=&\frac{\lambda}{1+S_{i}}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{v}}+{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{u}}]\\
&+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{(1+S_{i})^{2}}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}.\end{split}

The first term can be bounded as follows with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
:

\begin{split}\lambda\mathbb{E}_{i}[&|{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{v}}|]\\
\leq&C_{*}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}\|_{op}]\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{3}(n)}{n}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op},\end{split}

where we use the fact that
{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\preceq\lambda^{-1},{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\preceq 1
and where we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 1)
. The bound for
\mathbb{E}_{i-1}
holds in the same way without the factor
\log(n).

For the second term, we have that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}\lambda^{2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}&{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|]\\
\leq&\lambda^{2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{v}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})]\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}_{i}\|_{op}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i})]\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\|{\bm{u}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op},\end{split}

where in the last step we use
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 1)
. The bound for
\mathbb{E}_{i-1}
holds in the same way without the factor
\log(n).
Following the same steps described in Appendix
C.1
, we obtain that, with probability
1-n^{-D},

\begin{split}|{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}})-\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}})]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\tilde{\Psi}_{3}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}}).\end{split}

Appendix D
Proof of Lemma
1

Proof of Lemma
1
.

Bound the term
\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle

We first upper bound:

\begin{split}|\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle|\leq&|\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle-\mathbb{E}[\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle]|\\
&+|\mathbb{E}[\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle]|,\end{split}

and bound the martingale part and the deterministic part separately.

For the martingale part, we follow the same procedure described in Section
C.1
, and define:

\begin{split}\Delta_{i}=(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1})\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle,\end{split}

where
\mathbb{E}_{i}
is the expectation w.r.t
\{{\bm{x}}_{i+1},\dots,{\bm{x}}_{n}\},\{u_{i+1},\dots,u_{n}\}.

We have with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
that :

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\Delta_{i}^{2}]^{1/2}&\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[(\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle)^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&=2\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[(\langle\tilde{\bm{v}},{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle)^{2}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[(\langle\tilde{\bm{v}},{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{v}}\rangle)^{2}]^{1/4}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\|{\bm{u}}_{-i}\|_{2}^{4}]^{1/4}\\
&\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\|{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\|_{op}^{2}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2}^{4}]^{1/4}\mathbb{E}_{i-1}[\|{\bm{u}}_{-i}\|_{2}^{4}]^{1/4}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)\log^{1/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{n}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\log^{1/2}(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2},\end{split}

where we use
\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{u}}\|^{q}_{2}]^{1/q}\leq C_{*}\sqrt{n}.

Next, we prove high-probability bound on
\Delta_{i}.
Recall that

\Delta_{i}=(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1})(\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle-\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{-i}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{-i}\rangle)

We compute the martingale difference below:

\begin{split}&\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle-\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{-i}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{-i}\rangle\\
=&\frac{{\bm{v}}^{\top}({\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{-i}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{x}}_{i}}{1+{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\top}({\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{-i}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{x}}_{i}}(u_{i}-{\bm{x}}_{i}^{\top}({\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{X}}_{-i}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{-i})\\
=&\frac{\tilde{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}{1+{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}(u_{i}-{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}{\bm{u}}_{-i}),\end{split}

and upper bound with probability
1-n^{-D}
:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|\tilde{\bm{v}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq&\mathbb{E}_{i}[|\tilde{\bm{v}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{4}]^{1/4}\mathbb{E}[|u_{i}|^{4}]^{1/4}\\
\leq&C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\rm Tr}(\tilde{\bm{v}}\tilde{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2})^{2}]^{\frac{1}{4}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\end{split}

Further, we note that:

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{i}[|u_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq C_{*}\log(n)\\
&\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}{\bm{u}}_{-i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{Z}}_{-i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}\|_{op}\|{\bm{u}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}]^{1/2}\leq C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n),\end{split}

which implies with probability at least
1-n^{-D},

|\Delta_{i}|\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{-i}{\bm{u}}_{-i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n^{3/2}}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2},

and thus implies the bounds for the martingale part:

\begin{split}|\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle-\mathbb{E}[\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\end{split}

To bound the deterministic part, we note by symmetry that:

\mathbb{E}[\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle]=n\mathbb{E}[\frac{\tilde{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}}{1+{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}].

Next we note that:

\begin{split}&\big|n\mathbb{E}[\frac{\tilde{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}}{1+{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}]-n\mathbb{E}[\frac{\tilde{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}}{1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i})}]\big|\\
\leq&C_{*}n\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}))^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}_{i}^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i})^{3}]^{1/3}\\
\leq&C_{*}n\cdot\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
=&C_{*}\cdot\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\end{split}

Finally, note that
\mathbb{E}[\frac{\tilde{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}}{1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i})}]=0,
we have:

|\mathbb{E}[\langle{\bm{v}},({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle]|\leq C_{*}\cdot\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\tilde{\bm{v}}\|_{2}

Bound the term
|\langle{\bm{v}},{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{B}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle

We bound the martingale and the deterministic part similarly, and we only state the differences. First note that
\langle{\bm{v}},{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{B}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle=\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}},
and we compute the leave-one-out different as

\begin{split}&\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}-\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\\
=&-\frac{1}{1+\kappa_{i}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\\
&-\frac{1}{1+\kappa_{i}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\\
&+\frac{1}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\\
&+\frac{1}{1+\kappa_{i}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\\
&-\frac{1}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\\
&+\frac{1}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}\end{split}

where we define:

\begin{split}\hat{\bm{v}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{v}},\quad\tilde{\bm{B}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1/2},\quad\kappa_{i}={\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}\end{split}

For the martingale part, we upper bound the martingale difference
\Delta_{i}\coloneqq(\mathbb{E}_{i}-\mathbb{E}_{i-1})(\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}-\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}).
We have the following upper bound with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}_{i}[(\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}\hat{\bm{v}})]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{u}}_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bm{M}}_{i}\|_{op}^{3}]^{1/2},\\
&\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{u}}_{i}\|_{2}^{4}]^{1/4}\\
&\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{3/2}}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op},\\
\mathbb{E}_{i}[|\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}\hat{\bm{v}}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\mathbb{E}_{i}[{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}(n)\\
\mathbb{E}_{i}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq&\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\end{split}

where we use
\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{u}}_{i}\|_{2}^{q}]^{1/q}\leq\sqrt{n},
and
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4)
.

Combining the above bounds, we have with probability at least
1-n^{-D}

\begin{split}|\Delta_{i}|\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\end{split}

Further, using the same arguments, it is easy to see:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[|\Delta_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq C_{*}\log_{\beta}(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2},\end{split}

which implies that:

\begin{split}|\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}]|\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\end{split}

For the deterministic part, we first write:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}]=&n\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]\\
=&n\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{1+\kappa_{i}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]\\
&-n\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]\\
&+n\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}],\end{split}

and further bound:

\begin{split}&n\big|\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{1+\kappa_{i}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i})}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]\big|\\
\leq&C_{*}n\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}))^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[(\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i})^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[|u_{i}|^{3}]^{1/3}\\
\leq&C_{*}n\cdot\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}=\frac{C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}^{3}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
&n\big|\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]\big|\\
\leq&n\big|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]\big|+n|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]|\\
=&n\big|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]\big|\\
\leq&n\mathbb{E}[|\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[({\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}))^{3}]^{1/3}\mathbb{E}[u_{i}^{3}]^{1/3}\\
\leq&C_{*}n\cdot\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n^{3/2}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}=C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n^{1/2}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\\
&n\big|\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]\big|\\
\leq&n\big|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]\big|+n\big|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]\big|\\
\leq&n\big|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]\big|\\
\leq&C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n^{1/2}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2},\end{split}

where we use:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]=0,\quad\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{z}}_{i}{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})u_{i}]=0.\end{split}

Finally, by using that
\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i})}\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}u_{i}]=0,
we have:

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[\hat{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}]|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n)}{n^{1/2}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\|\hat{\bm{v}}\|_{2}\end{split}

Bound the term
\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{B}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle

We first note that
\langle{\bm{u}},{\bm{X}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{B}}({\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{X}}+\lambda)^{-1}{\bm{X}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}\rangle={\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}.
Then we follow the same steps as in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof Lemma 9 eq.(170), Proof Lemma 10 eq.(177))
, and we only state the difference here.

For the martingale part, we first compute the leave-one-out difference as:

\begin{split}&{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}-{\bm{u}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\\
=&\frac{{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}(u_{i}-{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i})^{2}-\frac{2}{1+\kappa_{i}}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}\end{split}

For terms involving
\tilde{\bm{B}},
recall that we have:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho^{2}_{\lambda}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\\
\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq\frac{C_{*}\log(n)\rho_{\lambda}^{3/2}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op},\end{split}

and the rest follows exactly the same as
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof Lemma 9 eq.(170))
, and we obtained that:

\begin{split}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}-\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}]\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{3}\log^{7/2}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\end{split}

For the deterministic part, we have:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{M}}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}]=n\mathbb{E}[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}u_{i}(u_{i}-{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i})-\frac{1}{1+\kappa_{i}}u_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}].\end{split}

We use

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i})|]\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{3}(n)}{n^{3/2}}\|{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\\
\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}|^{2}]^{1/2}\leq\frac{C_{*}\log\rho_{\lambda}^{3/2}(n)}{n}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\end{split}

and following the same steps as in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Proof Lemma 9 eq.(177))
, we obtain that:

\begin{split}n\left|\mathbb{E}[\frac{{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}}{(1+\kappa_{i})^{2}}u_{i}(u_{i}-{\bm{z}}_{i}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i})-\frac{1}{1+\kappa_{i}}u_{i}{\bm{z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{M}}_{i}\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}{\bm{Z}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{u}}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\frac{{\rm Tr}(\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2})}{(1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}))^{2}}]\right|\leq\frac{C_{*}\log\rho_{\lambda}^{5}(n)}{\sqrt{n}}\|\tilde{\bm{B}}\|_{op}\end{split}

Finally, by
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Proposition 6)
, we have:

\begin{split}|\mathbb{E}[\frac{{\rm Tr}(\tilde{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}_{i}^{2})}{(1+{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{i}^{2}))^{2}}]-\psi_{2}(\lambda_{*};{\bm{A}})|\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}^{6}(n)}{n^{3/2}}\end{split}

∎

Appendix E
Technical lemmas

We first prove an auxiliary lemma, which is a generalization of
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 2)
to non-PSD matrix.

Lemma 2
.

Assume
{\bm{x}}_{1},{\bm{x}}_{2}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are independent and satisfy the same assumptions in Theorem
5
. Then, for any constant
D>0,
there exist
C_{x,D}
such that for all matrix
{\bm{B}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
independent of
{\bm{x}}_{1},{\bm{x}}_{2}
we have with probability at least
1-n^{-D}

\begin{split}&|{\bm{x}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{B}}{\bm{x}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{B}})|\leq C_{x,D}\log(n)\left\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{F}.\end{split}

Moreover, for all integer
q,
there exist
C_{x,q}
such that for all
{\bm{B}}
independent of
{\bm{x}}_{1},{\bm{x}}_{1},
we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[|{\bm{x}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{B}}{\bm{x}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{B}})|^{q}]^{1/q}\leq C_{x,q}\left\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{F}.\end{split}

Proof of Lemma
2
.

For any
{\bm{B}},
we denote
{\bm{A}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{{\bm{B}}+{\bm{B}}^{\top}}{2}\right){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}},
and we have
{\bm{x}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{B}}{\bm{x}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{B}})={\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}),
where we define
{\bm{z}}_{1}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{x}}_{1}
and use
{\bm{x}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{B}}{\bm{x}}_{1}={\bm{x}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{B}}{\bm{x}}_{1}
and
{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{B}})={\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}{\bm{B}}^{\top})
as
{\bm{\Sigma}}
is symmetric.

Thus, it is sufficient to show the above bound for
{\bm{A}},
and we note that
{\bm{A}}
is symmetric. Define the eigen-decomposition of
{\bm{A}}
as
{\bm{A}}={\bm{U}}{\bm{\Lambda}}{\bm{U}}^{\top},
with
{\bm{\Lambda}}=\text{Diag}(\lambda_{i},\dots,\lambda_{d})
. We define the positive part and the negative part of
{\bm{A}}
as

\begin{split}&{\bm{\Lambda}}_{+}=\text{Diag}((\lambda_{i}\vee 0)_{i=1}^{d}),\qquad{\bm{A}}_{+}={\bm{U}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{+}{\bm{U}}^{\top},\\
&{\bm{\Lambda}}_{-}=-\text{Diag}((\lambda_{i}\wedge 0)_{i=1}^{d}),\qquad{\bm{A}}_{-}={\bm{U}}{\bm{\Lambda}}_{-}{\bm{U}}^{\top},\end{split}

where we have

\begin{split}{\bm{A}}={\bm{A}}_{+}-{\bm{A}}_{-},\qquad{\bm{A}}_{+},{\bm{A}}_{-}\succeq 0.\end{split}

We have that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}|{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}})|&\leq|{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{A}}_{+}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}_{+})|+|{\bm{z}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{A}}_{-}{\bm{z}}_{1}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}_{-})|\\
&\leq C_{x,D}\log(n)(\|{\bm{A}}_{+}\|_{F}+\|{\bm{A}}_{-}\|_{F})\\
&\leq C_{x,D}\log(n)\|{\bm{A}}\|_{F},\end{split}

where we use
\|{\bm{A}}_{+}\|_{F},\|{\bm{A}}_{-}\|_{F}\leq\|{\bm{A}}\|_{F}.

For the bound in expectation, we integrate the tail bound in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Lemma 2)
and we get

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}[|{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{x}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}})|^{q}]=&q\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{q-1}\mathbb{P}\left(|{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{z}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{B}})|\geq t\right)\,\differential t\\
\leq&q\int_{0}^{\infty}t^{q-1}\left(\mathbb{P}\left(|{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}_{+}{\bm{z}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}_{+})|\geq\frac{t}{2}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(|{\bm{z}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}_{-}{\bm{z}}-{\rm Tr}({\bm{A}}_{-})|\geq\frac{t}{2}\right)\right)\,\differential t\\
\leq&C_{x,q}(\|{\bm{A}}_{+}\|_{F}^{q}+\|{\bm{A}}_{-}\|_{F}^{q})\\
\leq&C_{x,q}\|{\bm{A}}\|_{F}^{q},\end{split}

where again in the last inequality we use
\|{\bm{A}}_{+}\|_{F},\|{\bm{A}}_{-}\|_{F}\leq\|{\bm{A}}\|_{F}
.

Finally, we note that
\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{F}=\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{B}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{F},
which implies that
\|{\bm{A}}\|_{F}\leq\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{F}
and concludes the proof.

∎

Lemma 3
.

Under the same assumptions in Theorem
6
, recall

\mu_{*}=\frac{n}{1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}})},\quad\tilde{\mu}_{-}=\frac{n}{1+\mathbb{E}[{\rm Tr}({\bm{M}}_{-})]}.

Then, we have the following properties

\begin{split}\frac{n}{2\rho_{\lambda}(n)}\leq\mu_{*}\leq n,\quad\tilde{\mu}_{-}\leq\left(1+C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{5/2}\varphi_{1}(d)/n\right)\mu_{*}.\end{split}

Proof.

The first inequality directly follows from
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 2)
using also that
1+{\rm Tr}(\overline{{\bm{M}}})\leq\rho_{\lambda}^{2}(n),
and the second one follows from
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Claim 3)
.
∎

Lemma 4
.

Under the same assumptions in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Lemma 4)
, for any p.s.d. matrix
{\bm{B}}
,
\ell\geq 1
and
q>1
, we have that, with probability at least
1-n^{-D}
,

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}\|_{op}^{q}]^{1/q}\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{\ell}}{n^{\ell-1}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}.\end{split}

Proof.

By
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof Lemma 4.(b))
, denoting
\mathcal{A}
to be the event on which
{\bm{M}}\preceq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n},
we have
\Pr[\mathcal{A}]\geq 1-n^{-D^{\prime}}.
Furthermore, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}\|_{op}^{q}]^{1/q}&\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}\|_{op}^{q}1_{\mathcal{A}}]^{1/q}+\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}\|_{op}^{q}1_{\mathcal{A}^{c}}]^{1/q}.\end{split}

For the first part, we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}\|_{op}^{q}1_{\mathcal{A}}]^{1/q}&\leq\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{\ell-1}\|_{op}\|{\bm{M}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{q}1_{\mathcal{A}}]^{1/q}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{\ell-1}}{n^{\ell-1}}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}^{q}1_{\mathcal{A}}]^{1/q}\\
&\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{\ell}}{n^{\ell-1}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op},\end{split}

where we use
\|{\bm{M}}\|_{op}\leq C_{*}\frac{\rho_{\lambda}(n)}{n},
and for the last step we define
{\bm{v}}
to be the eigenvalue of
{\bm{B}}^{1/2}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}^{1/2}
of unit norm and obtain:

\begin{split}\|{\bm{M}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{1/2}\|_{op}=&\|{\bm{B}}^{1/2}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}^{1/2}\|_{op}\\
=&{\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{1/2}{\bm{M}}{\bm{B}}^{1/2}{\bm{v}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}(n){\bm{v}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{1/2}\overline{{\bm{M}}}{\bm{B}}^{1/2}{\bm{v}}\\
\leq&C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}(n)\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}.\end{split}

For the second part, we use the same argument as in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Lemma 4)
, and we have

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}_{i}[\|{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{M}}^{\ell/2}\|_{op}^{q}1_{\mathcal{A}^{c}}]^{1/q}\leq&\frac{\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\ell/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\ell/2}\|_{op}}{\lambda^{\ell}}\mathbb{E}[1_{\mathcal{A}^{c}}]^{1/q}\\
\leq&\frac{\|{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{1/2}\|_{op}}{\lambda^{\ell}}\mathbb{E}[1_{\mathcal{A}^{c}}]^{1/q}\\
\leq&(\mu_{*}+\lambda)\frac{\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op}}{\lambda^{\ell}}\\
\leq&\frac{C_{*}\rho_{\lambda}(n)^{\ell}}{n^{\ell-1}}\|\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}{\bm{B}}\overline{{\bm{M}}}^{1/2}\|_{op},\end{split}

where the last step follows form the same steps in
(
Misiakiewicz & Saeed 2024
, Proof of Lemma 4.(b))
, by picking
r=\ell-1.

∎

Appendix F
Technical results for scaling laws

F.1
Approximation of the trace

We give a generalization of the estimation in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Appendix D)
. We have the conditions:

s_{1}+s_{2}\in\{0,1\},0\leq\delta_{1}\leq\gamma_{1},0\leq\delta_{2}\leq\gamma_{2}.

Define

T(\mu,\nu;(s_{1},\delta_{1},\gamma_{1}),(s_{2},\delta_{2},\gamma_{2}))=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{k^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\mu)^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{k^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\nu)^{\gamma_{2}}},

and drop the script
(s_{1},\delta_{1},\gamma_{1}),(s_{2},\delta_{2},\gamma_{2})
when the notation is clear. We assume
s_{1}+s_{2}+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})>1
(otherwise the sum does not converge), noting that in all cases considered later (see Lemma
5
) this condition is satisfied. We consider
\mu=o_{n_{\mathsf{t}}}(1)
,
\nu=o_{n_{\mathsf{t}}}(1)
and split the analysis into two cases:
\mu\propto\nu
and
\mu\ll\nu
. The case
\nu\ll\mu
is analogous to
\mu\ll\nu
.

Case 1:
\mu\propto\nu
.

We assume there exists a finite
C
that does not depend on
n_{\mathsf{t}}
such that
\nu=C\mu.
Then,

\begin{split}T(\mu,\nu)&=\mu^{-(\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2})+(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})+\frac{(s_{1}+s_{2})}{\alpha}}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(k\mu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{((k\mu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{(k\mu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{((k\mu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-\alpha}+C)^{\gamma_{2}}}\\
&=\mu^{-(\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2})+(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})+\frac{(s_{1}+s_{2}-1)}{\alpha}}\left(\int_{\mu^{1/\alpha}}^{+\infty}\frac{x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+C)^{\gamma_{2}}}\,\differential x+err\right),\end{split}

where
err=O(\mu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})=o_{n_{\mathsf{t}}}(1).

Since

\int_{\mu^{1/\alpha}}^{+\infty}\frac{x^{-(s_{1}+s_{2})-(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+(1\vee C))^{\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}}\,\differential x\leq\int_{\mu^{1/\alpha}}^{+\infty}\frac{x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+C)^{\gamma_{2}}}\,\differential x\leq\int_{\mu^{1/\alpha}}^{+\infty}\frac{x^{-(s_{1}+s_{2})-(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+(1\wedge C))^{\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}}}\,\differential x,

by applying the same arguments as in
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Equation 175)
, we have

\begin{split}T(\mu,\nu)=\Theta(\mu^{[\frac{1}{\alpha}(s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2}))]\wedge 0}).\end{split}

Case 2:
\mu\ll\nu
.

First of all, it is easy to see that

\begin{split}\Theta(\nu^{[\frac{1}{\alpha}(s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2}))]\wedge 0})\leq T(\mu,\nu)\leq\Theta(\mu^{[\frac{1}{\alpha}(s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2}))]\wedge 0}).\end{split}

Thus, if
s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2})>0,
we have

T(\mu,\nu)=\Theta(1),

and it remains to handle the case where
s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2})<0.
In this case, we have

\begin{split}T(\mu,\nu)&=\nu^{-(\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2})+(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})+\frac{(s_{1}+s_{2})}{\alpha}}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{(k\nu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{((k\nu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-\alpha}+\nu^{-1}\mu)^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{(k\nu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{((k\nu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}\\
&=\nu^{-(\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2})+(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})+\frac{(s_{1}+s_{2}-1)}{\alpha}}\left(\int_{\nu^{1/\alpha}}^{+\infty}\frac{x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+\nu^{-1}\mu)^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}\,\differential x+err_{1}\right)\\
&=\nu^{-(\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2})+(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2})+\frac{(s_{1}+s_{2}-1)}{\alpha}}\left(\int_{\nu^{1/\alpha}}^{+\infty}\frac{x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha})^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}\,\differential x+err_{1}+err_{2}\right),\end{split}

where

err_{1}=O(\nu^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})=o_{n_{\mathsf{t}}}(1),\qquad err_{2}=O(\nu^{-1}\mu)=o_{n_{\mathsf{t}}}(1).

The bound on
err_{1}
follows from the standard approximation of the Riemann integral by a Riemann sum. To bound
err_{2},
we apply the mean value theorem to
(\cdot)^{-\gamma_{1}}
:

\begin{split}&\left|\frac{x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+\nu^{-1}\mu)^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}-\frac{x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha})^{\gamma_{1}}}\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}\right|\\
&\qquad=x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}\left|(x^{-\alpha}+\nu^{-1}\mu)^{-\gamma_{1}}-(x^{-\alpha})^{-\gamma_{1}}\right|\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}\\
&\qquad=x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}\left|-\gamma_{1}(x^{-\alpha}+\theta_{x}\nu^{-1}\mu)^{-\gamma_{1}-1}\right|\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}\nu^{-1}\mu,\end{split}

where
\theta_{x}\in[0,1]
. Since
s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2})<0,
which implies
s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-(\gamma_{1}+1)-\gamma_{2})<0,
we have

\int_{\nu^{1/\alpha}}^{+\infty}x^{-s_{1}-\delta_{1}\alpha}\left|-\gamma_{1}(x^{-\alpha}+\theta_{x}\nu^{-1}\mu)^{-\gamma_{1}-1}\right|\frac{x^{-s_{2}-\delta_{2}\alpha}}{(x^{-\alpha}+1)^{\gamma_{2}}}\,\differential x=O(1),

which implies
err_{2}=O(\nu^{-1}\mu)\ll 1
.

Combining the two regimes, we have

T(\mu,\nu;(s_{1},\delta_{1},\gamma_{1}),(s_{2},\delta_{2},\gamma_{2}))=\Theta\left((\mu\vee\nu)^{[\frac{1}{\alpha}(s_{1}+s_{2}-1+\alpha(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}-\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2}))]\wedge 0}\right)=\Theta\left(T^{s_{1}+s_{2}}_{(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}),(\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2})}(\mu\vee\nu)\right),

where the definition of
T^{s}_{\delta,\gamma}(\cdot)
corresponds to
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Appendix D)
.

F.2
Technical lemmas

Lemma 5
.

We have the following approximations regarding the traces and inner products appearing in the expression of the test error:

\begin{split}&{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}},\\
&{\rm Tr}(({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+2\alpha)}{\alpha}},\\
&{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}},\\
&{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}},\\
&{\rm Tr}(({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+3\alpha)}{\alpha}},\\
&{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+2\alpha)}{\alpha}},\\
&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{(2r-2)\wedge 0},\\
&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=T_{2r,1}^{1}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})=\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{(2r-1)\wedge 0},\\
&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-1}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{(2r-1)\wedge 0},\\
&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}^{3}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{(2r-1)\wedge 0},\\
&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle\approx\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{(2r-2)\wedge 0},\\
&\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle\approx\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{(2r-1)\wedge 0}.\end{split}

Proof.

We apply the approximation in Appendix
F.1
and obtain

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})=T_{3,3}^{0}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Tr}(({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})=T_{1,3}^{0}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+2\alpha)}{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})=T_{2,3}^{0}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})=T_{3,4}^{0}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Tr}(({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})=T_{1,4}^{0}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+3\alpha)}{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\rm{\displaystyle Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})=T_{2,4}^{0}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\wedge\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+2\alpha)}{\alpha}},

\displaystyle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{k^{-1-2(r+1)\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{2}(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{2}}=T_{2r+2,4}^{1}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{(2r-2)\wedge 0},

\displaystyle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{k^{-1-2r\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})}=T_{2r,1}^{1}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})\approx\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{(2r-1)\wedge 0},

\displaystyle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-1}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{k^{-1-(2r+1)\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})}=T_{2r+1,2}^{1}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{(2r-1)\wedge 0},

\displaystyle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}^{3}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{k^{-1-(2r+3)\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{2}(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{2}}=T_{2r+3,4}^{1}((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}))\approx(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{(2r-1)\wedge 0},

\displaystyle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{k^{-1-2r\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{2}}=T_{2r,2}^{1}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\approx\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{(2r-2)\wedge 0},

\displaystyle\langle{\bm{\beta}}_{*},{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\rangle=\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{k^{-1-(2r+1)\alpha}}{(k^{-\alpha}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{2}}=T_{2r+1,2}^{1}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\approx\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{(2r-1)\wedge 0}.

∎

Next, we compute the scaling of
\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2},\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}),\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}),\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2},\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}),\chi_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}).

Lemma 6
.

We have the following scaling of the quantities appearing in the expression of the test error:

\begin{split}&\begin{cases}&\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\approx n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-\alpha\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}}}{\alpha}\wedge 1\wedge\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{t}}}\right)}\\
&\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}\approx\begin{cases}n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-\alpha}\text{ if }1<(\frac{1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}}}{\alpha}\wedge\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{t}}})\\
n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-(1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}})},\quad\text{else}.\end{cases}\end{cases}\qquad\begin{cases}&\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}\approx n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-\alpha\left(\frac{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\alpha\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}\wedge 1\wedge\frac{\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}\right)}\\
&\mu_{\mathsf{s},1}\approx\begin{cases}n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-\alpha}\text{ if }1<\frac{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\alpha\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}\wedge\frac{\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}\\
n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-\frac{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}},\quad\text{else}.\end{cases}\end{cases}\\
&\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\approx n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-1+(\frac{1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}}}{\alpha}\wedge 1\wedge\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{t}}})},\qquad\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})\approx n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1+(\frac{1+\frac{\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}}{\alpha}\wedge 1\wedge\frac{\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}})},\\
&\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\approx p_{\mathsf{t}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{-1-\frac{1}{\alpha}},\qquad\chi_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})\approx p_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-1-\frac{1}{\alpha}}.\end{split}

Proof.

From
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Appendix D)
and using the relation
p_{\mathsf{s}}=n_{\mathsf{s}}^{\frac{\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}},\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}=n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-\frac{\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}}
, we have

\begin{split}&\begin{cases}&\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}=\frac{\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}T_{11}^{0}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})+\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}\\
&\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}=\frac{\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}T_{11}^{0}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})+\frac{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\end{cases}\qquad\begin{cases}&\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}=\frac{\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}}{p_{\mathsf{s}}}T_{11}^{0}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})+\mu_{\mathsf{s},1}\\
&\mu_{\mathsf{s},1}=\frac{\mu_{\mathsf{s},1}}{n_{\mathsf{s}}}T_{11}^{0}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})+\frac{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}{n_{\mathsf{s}}}\end{cases}\\
&\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\approx n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-1+(\frac{1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}}}{\alpha}\wedge 1\wedge\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{t}}})},\qquad\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})\approx n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1+(\frac{1+\frac{\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}}}{\alpha}\wedge 1\wedge\frac{\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}})},\\
&\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},1},\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})\approx p_{\mathsf{t}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}^{-1-\frac{1}{\alpha}},\qquad\chi_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})\approx p_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-1-\frac{1}{\alpha}}.\end{split}

Then, directly from
(
Defilippis et al. 2024
, Appendix D)
we get

\begin{split}&\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}\approx\begin{cases}n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-\alpha}\text{ if }1<(\frac{1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}}}{\alpha}\wedge\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{t}}})\\
n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-(1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}})},\quad\text{else}.\end{cases},\qquad\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\approx n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-\alpha\left(\frac{1+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{t}}}}{\alpha}\wedge 1\wedge\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{t}}}\right)},\end{split}

and by further applying the same analysis to
\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}
, the result readily follows.
∎

Next, we compute
{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0},\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}),\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}).

Lemma 7
.

We have the following scaling of the quantities appearing in the expression of the test error:

\begin{split}&{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\approx{\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}+n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}+p_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2},\\
&\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0})\approx\frac{(1-\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-1})}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\left((\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}+n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+2\alpha)}{\alpha}}+p_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{(\alpha+1)}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}}\right),\\
&\chi_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0})\approx\frac{1}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}\left[(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}}+n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+3\alpha)}{\alpha}}+p_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}(\mu_{\mathsf{t},2}\vee\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-\frac{(1+2\alpha)}{\alpha}}\right].\end{split}

Proof.

We first recall that

\displaystyle{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}+\frac{\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})}{1-\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}+\frac{\chi_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})}{1-\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}(\mu_{\mathsf{s},1},\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}+\bar{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}.

From Lemma
6
, we know that
\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}\approx n_{\mathsf{t}}^{-\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}+\left(\frac{\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}+\gamma_{\lambda_{\mathsf{s}}}}{\alpha}\wedge\gamma_{n_{\mathsf{s}}}\wedge\gamma_{p_{\mathsf{s}}}\right)},\chi_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}\approx p_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}}.
Thus, we know that
1-\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{s}},p_{\mathsf{s}}}=\Theta(1).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle{\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}\approx

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}+n_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}+p_{\mathsf{s}}^{-1}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{-\frac{(1+\alpha)}{\alpha}}\mu_{\mathsf{s},2}^{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{s},2})^{-2}.

Now we compute
\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0})
:

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{n_{\mathsf{t}},p_{\mathsf{t}}}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{\mathsf{t}}}\left[{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-1})-\mu_{\mathsf{t},1}\frac{{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Lambda}}_{0}{\bm{\Sigma}}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}{1-\frac{1}{p_{\mathsf{t}}}{\rm Tr}({\bm{\Sigma}}^{2}({\bm{\Sigma}}+\mu_{\mathsf{t},2})^{-2})}\right]
